# The coset table of the basis A = B_P · T_X of H(G34), P = G33 = <s1, s2, s3, s4, s5>: the setRow program (written by programs/bfs_basis.jl)
# 756 cosets P x_n, X = T_o(P\W), o = (1,2,3,4,5,6), z = letter 7 (no columns of z)
# complete: true; 10 sweeps, 755 tree edges, 7552 setRow's (found modulo p = 15013 at e1 = 11; executed exactly by programs/bmm_proof.jl G34)
# makeEdge(n, m, g): x_m = x_n g, so x_n T_g = x_m and x_m T_g^-1 = x_n;  setRow(n, g, 0, gen|inv): row (n, g) from the Hecke relation of |g|;
# setRow(n, g, [w], rel): row (n, g) = x_n T_w for the braid-group identity g = w (a rotation of a relator);
# the rows x_1 T_s^±1 = T_s^±1 x_1 (s in P) are set at the construction of the table.  Coefficients are in H(P), on the left.
